elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n            \n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n            \n            // Add flux from all connected elements\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable world;\n    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);\n    \n    // Calculate memory usage\n    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n    const size_t dynamic_mem        total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running duration_ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration_ms);\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);\n    const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;\n    const double giga_elems_per_sec = (n_measured_iters * n_elems) / (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    \n    // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)\n    const double gflops = giga_elems_per_sec * 22.0;\n    \n    printf(\"Performance:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\\n\", time_per_iter);\n    printf(\"  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\\n\", giga_elems_per_sec);\n    printf(\"  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Compute hash for verification\n    const uint64_t hash = computeHash(world.elements_dynamic);\n    printf(\"  Result hash: %016lX\\n\", hash);\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        std::vector<double> energyData;\n        energyData.reserve(world.elements_dynamic.size());\n        for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n            energyData.push_back(elem.current_energy);\n        }\n        print_results(energyData, \"ElementEnergy\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        bool valid = validateResults(world);\n        if (!valid) {\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n======\n#pragma once\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <cinttypes>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"hash.hpp\"\n\n// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum\ntemplate <typename T>\nT kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = Each rank\n// stores its owned rows plus one halo row before and one after. The local\n// element numbering is\n//     local_idx = (x - x_begin + 1) * n_elems_root + y\n// so that halo rows occupy local rows 0 and n_local_rows + 1. Because the halo\n// rows are contiguous in memory at both ends of the dynamic buffers, halo\n// exchange is done with zero packing directly out of / into those buffers.\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n\n    // prev_rank = MPI_PROC_NULL;  // Rank owning row x_begin - 1\n    int next_rank = MPI_PROC_NULL;  // Rank owning row x_begin + n_local_rows\n\n    size_t n_owned() const { return static_cast<size_t>(n_local_rows) * n_elems_root; }\n    size_t owned_begin() const { return static_cast<size_t>(n_elems_root); }  // local index of first owned element\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Number of rows owned by a given rank for an even block distribution\ninline int rowsForRank(const int n_elems_root, const int rank, const int size) {\n    return n_elems_root / size + (rank < n_elems_root % size ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\ninline int rowBeginForRank(const int n_elems_root, const int rank, const int size) {\n    const int base = n_elems_root / size;\n    const int rem = n_elems_root % size;\n    return rank * base + std::min(rank, rem);\n}\n\n// Build (the local partition of) a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root, const int rank, const int size) {\n    world.n_elems_root = n_elems_root;\n    world.n_local_rows = rowsForRank(n_elems_root, rank, size);\n    world.x_begin = rowBeginForRank(n_elems_root, rank, size);\n\n    // Neighbouring ranks (only if they own at least one row)\n    world.prev_rank = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    world.next_rank = MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    if (world.n_local_rows > 0) {\n        if (rank > 0 && rowsForRank(n_elems_root, rank - 1, size) > 0) world.prev_rank = rank - 1;\n        if (rank + 1 < size && rowsForRank(n_elems_root, rank + 1, size) > 0) world.next_rank = rank + 1;\n    }\n\n    const size_t n_local = static_cast<size_t>(world.n_local_rows + 2) * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements (owned + 2 halo rows)\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_local);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_local);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_local);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_local; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each owned element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int lx = 0; lx < world.n_local_rows; ++lx) {\n        const int x = world.x_begin + lx;\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(lx + 1) * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const size_t neighbor_idx =\n                        static_cast<size_t>(nx - world.x_begin + 1) * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    auto setMaterial = [&](const int x, const int y, const idx_t mat) {\n        if (x >= world.x_begin && x < world.x_begin + world.n_local_rows) {\n            const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(x - world.x_begin + 1) * n_elems_root + y;\n            world.elements_static[idx].material_idx = mat;\n        }\n    };\n    setMaterial(0, 0, INFLOW_MAT_ID);\n    setMaterial(0, last, OUTFLOW_MAT_ID);\n    setMaterial(last, 0, OUTFLOW_MAT_ID);\n    setMaterial(last, last, INFLOW_MAT_ID);\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update a contiguous range [begin, end) of local elements\ninline void updateRange(const World& world, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict es = world.elements_static.data();\n    const ElementDynamic* __restrict ed = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* __restrict ew = const_cast<ElementDynamic*>(world.elements_dynamic_swap.data());\n    const Material* __restrict mats = world.materials.data();\n\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& elem_static = es[i];\n        const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = ed[i];\n        const Material& mat = mats[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n        // Start with external flow\n        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n        // Add flux from all connected elements\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n            const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n            const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = ed[neighbor_idx];\n            total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n        }\n\n        // Update element state\n        ElementDynamic& elem_write = ew[i];\n        elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n        elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n    }\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n = world.n_elems_root;\n    const size_t n_rows = world.n_local_rows;\n    const size_t first = world.owned_begin();        // first owned local index\n    const size_t last = first + n_rows * n;          // one past last owned local index\n\n    // Only current_energy of halo elements is needed by neighbours, but exchanging\n    // whole ElementDynamic rows (16 B/elem) keeps the exchange zero-copy.\n    const int halo_count = static_cast<int>(n * sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        ElementDynamic* dyn = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n        MPI_Request reqs[4];\n        int n_reqs = 0;\n\n        if (n_rows > 0) {\n            // Receive halos from neighbours into the halo rows\n            MPI_Irecv(dyn, halo_count, MPI_BYTE, world.prev_rank, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[n_reqs++]);\n            MPI_Irecv(dyn + last, halo_count, MPI_BYTE, world.next_rank, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[n_reqs++]);\n            // Send our first/last owned rows\n            MPI_Isend(dyn + first, halo_count, MPI_BYTE, world.prev_rank, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[n_reqs++]);\n            MPI_Isend(dyn + last - n, halo_count, MPI_BYTE, world.next_rank, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[n_reqs++]);\n        }\n\n        if (n_rows > 2) {\n            // Interior rows do not depend on the halo: overlap with communication\n            updateRange(world, first + n, last - n);\n            MPI_Waitall(n_reqs, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n            updateRange(world, first, first + n);\n            updateRange(world, last - n, last);\n        } else {\n            MPI_Waitall(n_reqs, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n            updateRange(world, first, last);\n        }\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Gather the owned dynamic state of all ranks onto rank 0 in global order.\n// Returns the full array on rank 0, an empty vector elsewhere.\nstd::vector<ElementDynamic> gatherDynamic(const World& world, const int rank, const int size) {\n    std::vector<int> counts(size), displs(size);\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rowsForRank(world.n_elems_root, r, size) * world.n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(static_cast<size_t>(rowBeginForRank(world.n_elems_root, r, size)) * world.n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n    }\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> all;\n    if (rank == 0) all.resize(static_cast<size_t>(world.n_elems_root) * world.n_elems_root);\n    MPI_Gatherv(world.elements_dynamic.data() + world.owned_begin(),\n                static_cast<int>(world.n_owned() * sizeof(ElementDynamic)), MPI_BYTE,\n                all.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_BYTE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    return all;\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results (expects the globally gathered element array; rank 0 only)\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : elements) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n\n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification.\n// Each rank hashes its owned elements using their global indices; since the\n// combination is XOR, the partial hashes are reduced with MPI_BXOR to obtain\n// exactly the same value as the sequential hash over the global array.\nuint64_t computeHash(const World& world) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    const ElementDynamic* elements = world.elements_dynamic.data() + world.owned_begin();\n    const size_t n_owned = world.n_owned();\n    const size_t global_offset = static_cast<size_t>(world.x_begin) * world.n_elems_root;\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n_owned; ++k) {\n        const size_t i = global_offset + k;\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[k].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[k].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    uint64_t global_hash = 0;\n    MPI_Allreduce(&hash, &global_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    return global_hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n     validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (is_root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (n_elems_root < 0) n_elems_root = 0;\n\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS]; };\nstruct ElementDynamic { val_t current_energy; val_t total_flux; };\nstruct World { std::vector<Material> materials; std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static; std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic; std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap; };\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0, INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1, OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0}); world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5}); world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems); world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems); world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) { world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID; world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0; world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0; world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0; }\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n        const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y; ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n        const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n        for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) { const int nx = x + offsets[n][0]; const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n            if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) { elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = nx * n_elems_root + ny; elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0; elem.num_connections++; } }\n    }\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID; world.elements_static[last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID; world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& a, val_t cf, const ElementDynamic& b) { return (b.current_energy - a.current_energy) * mat.transfer_coeff * cf * 0.25; }\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& es = world.elements_static[i]; const ElementDynamic& ed = world.elements_dynamic[i]; const Material& mat = world.materials[es.material_idx];\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < es.num_connections; ++j) total_flux += computeFlux(mat, ed, es.connected_flux[j], world.elements_dynamic[es.connected_idx[j]]);\n            ElementDynamic& w = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i]; w.current_energy = ed.current_energy + total_flux; w.total_flux = ed.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0, flux_sum = 0.0, energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest(), energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    for (const auto& e : world.elements_dynamic) { energy_sum += e.current_energy; flux_sum += e.total_flux; energy_max = std::max(e.current_energy, energy_max); energy_min = std::min(e.current_energy, energy_min); }\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\"); printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum); printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum); printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > 1e-8) printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\"); return true;\n}\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& el) { uint64_t hash = 0; for (size_t i = 0; i < el.size(); ++i) { const uint64_t* e = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&el[i].current_energy); const uint64_t* f = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&el[i].total_flux); hash ^= (*e + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL; hash ^= (*f + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL; } return hash; }\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n = 512, it = 10; bool v = false, r = false;\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) { if (!strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\")) n = atoi(argv[++i]); else if (!strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\")) it = atoi(argv[++i]); else if (!strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\")) v = true; else if (!strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\")) r = true; }\n    World world; buildSquare2D(world, n);\n    auto s = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now(); runSimulation(world, it); auto e = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", (long)std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(e - s).count());\n    printf(\"  Result hash: %016lX\\n\", computeHash(world.elements_dynamic));\n    if (r) { std::vector<double> d; for (auto& x : world.elements_dynamic) d.push_back(x.current_energy); print_results(d, \"ElementEnergy\"); }\n    if (v) validateResults(world);\n}\nEOF\ng++ -O3 -march=native -std=c++20 ref/unstructured/u.cpp -o ref/u_ref && echo REF_BUILT\nmkdir build_mpi && cd build_mpi && cmake /home/llmtest/evals/20261011-092100/unstructured_claude-fable-5.1-cc-medium_mpi_r3/unstructured >/dev/null && make 2>&1 | tail -5","description":"Build reference sequential version and MPI version"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10627,"cache_read_input_tokens":24386,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":10627},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2a8f5a7b-7669-4943-92f4-daf7a4aefa09","uuid":"536fc8b5-8ef0-4fbb-b303-7f6956f74fa0","timestamp":"2026-10-25T00:43:38.312Z","request_id":"req_011CgN2b4TqjnEh4w4EFp84E"}

The element arrays are streamed through\n// every iteration, so cutting TLB misses matters; this also sidesteps the MPI\n// runtime's malloc tuning (it disables mmap-backed malloc), which otherwise\n// slows down large-array traversal noticeably.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct HugePageAllocator {\n    using value_type = T;\n    static constexpr size_t kHugePage = 2u << 20;\n\n    HugePageAllocator() = default;\n    template <typename U>\n    HugePageAllocator(const HugePageAllocator<U>&) noexcept {}\n\n    T* allocate(size_t n) {\n        const size_t bytes = (n * sizeof(T) + kHugePage - 1) / kHugePage * kHugePage;\n        if (bytes == 0) return nullptr;\n        // Over-allocate by one huge page so that the region can be aligned, then trim.\n        char* raw = static_cast<char*>(mmap(nullptr, bytes + kHugePage, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE,\n                                            MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0));\n        if (raw == MAP_FAILED) throw std::bad_alloc();\n        char* aligned = reinterpret_cast<char*>(\n            (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(raw) + kHugePage - 1) & ~(uintptr_t)(kHugePage - 1));\n        if (aligned != raw) munmap(raw, aligned - raw);\n        const size_t tail = (raw + bytes + kHugePage) - (aligned + bytes);\n        if (tail > 0) munmap(aligned + bytes, tail);\n        madvise(aligned, bytes, MADV_HUGEPAGE);\n        return reinterpret_cast<T*>(aligned);\n    }\n    void deallocate(T* p, size_t n) noexcept {\n        if (p == nullptr) return;\n        const size_t bytes = (n * sizeof(T) + kHugePage - 1) / kHugePage * kHugePage;\n        munmap(p, bytes);\n    }\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator==(const HugePageAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return true; }\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator!=(const HugePageAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return false; }\n};\n''')\ns=s.replace('''    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n\n    // Decomposition info''','''    std::vector<ElementStatic, HugePageAllocator<ElementStatic>> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic, HugePageAllocator<ElementDynamic>> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic, HugePageAllocator<ElementDynamic>> elements_dynamic_swap;\n\n    // Decomposition info''')\ns=s.replace('#include <limits>\\n','#include <limits>\\n#include <new>\\n')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncd /tmp/build_mpi && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\" ; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgN4VVst4XFVrMDwSQDT8","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The MPI parallelization is complete and verified. Only the two existing files changed, the executable is still named `unstructured`, and no new dependencies were added beyond MPI itself.\n\n**What changed in `unstructured/unstructured.cpp`**\n\n- **Row-block decomposition.** Each rank owns a contiguous block of grid rows plus one halo row on each side. Connectivity is built in local numbering, so the update kernel is unchanged in form and arithmetic order.\n- **Zero-copy halo exchange.** Halo rows sit at the two ends of the dynamic buffers, so nonblocking sends and receives work directly on the buffers. Interior rows are computed while the halo is in flight, then the two boundary rows after the wait.\n- **Exact hash.** Each rank hashes its elements with global indices and the partials are combined with an XOR reduction, which reproduces the sequential hash bit for bit.\n- **Validation and results printing** gather the full array to rank 0 only when the `-v` or `-r` flags are given, so the sums and samples use the original sequential order and match exactly. These are outside the timed region.\n- **Edge cases handled.** Ranks with zero or fewer than three rows, more ranks than rows, and exit codes broadcast so all ranks agree.\n- **A memory allocator** for the three element arrays using huge-page-advised mappings with an explicit local NUMA policy via the raw `mbind` syscall.\n\n**Why the allocator was needed.** With plain allocation, a single MPI rank ran about 20 percent slower than the original, and the unmodified reference showed the same slowdown once MPI_Init was called. Perf and vmstat showed the cause: Open MPI's helper threads make the kernel's automatic NUMA balancing scan the process, injecting 1.9 million hint page faults into the time loop. Giving the arrays an explicit policy makes the scanner skip them. After that, one rank matches the reference time exactly.\n\n**Verification.** Hashes, Kahan sums, min, max, samples, and validation output are identical to the sequential original for grids of 1, 2, 7, 100, and 512 on 1 to 13 ranks, including oversubscribed cases with more ranks than rows.\n\nStrong scaling on a 4096 by 4096 grid with 100 iterations on this 2-socket EPYC node, which was under external load during the runs:\n\n| Ranks | Time (ms) | Speedup |\n|---|---|---|\n| sequential original | 15865 | 1.0 |\n| 1 | 15771 | 1.0 |\n| 16 | 3658 | 4.3 |\n| 64 | 1334 | 11.9 |\n| 128 | 1148 | 13.8 |\n\nScaling flattens because the kernel streams 160 bytes per element per iteration and saturates the node's memory bandwidth at around 240 GB/s. Rank placement across NUMA nodes made no difference, and halo traffic is only 128 KB per rank per iteration, so the limit is bandwidth rather than communication. On a multi-node cluster, where each node adds its own memory bandwidth, this decomposition should continue to scale.\n\n**`unstructured/CMakeLists.txt`** now does `find_package(MPI REQUIRED)` and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`. Only the two existing files changed, the executable is still named `unstructured`, and no new dependencies were added beyond MPI itself.\n\n**What changed in `unstructured/unstructured.cpp`**\n\n- **Row-block decomposition.** Each rank owns a contiguous block of grid rows plus one halo row on each side. Connectivity is built in local numbering, so the update kernel is unchanged in form and arithmetic order.\n- **Zero-copy halo exchange.** Halo rows sit at the two ends of the dynamic buffers, so nonblocking sends and receives work directly on the buffers. Interior rows are computed while the halo is in flight, then the two boundary rows after the wait.\n- **Exact hash.** Each rank hashes its elements with global indices and the partials are combined with an XOR reduction, which reproduces the sequential hash bit for bit.\n- **Validation and results printing** gather the full array to rank 0 only when the `-v` or `-r` flags are given, so the sums and samples use the original sequential order and match exactly. These are outside the timed region.\n- **Edge cases handled.** Ranks with zero or fewer than three rows, more ranks than rows, and exit codes broadcast so all ranks agree.\n- **A memory allocator** for the three element arrays using huge-page-advised mappings with an explicit local NUMA policy via the raw `mbind` syscall.\n\n**Why the allocator was needed.** With plain allocation, a single MPI rank ran about 20 percent slower than the original, and the unmodified reference showed the same slowdown once MPI_Init was called. Perf and vmstat showed the cause: Open MPI's helper threads make the kernel's automatic NUMA balancing scan the process, injecting 1.9 million hint page faults into the time loop. Giving the arrays an explicit policy makes the scanner skip them.
